## Photorealistic Gym Selfie Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt_type": "photorealistic gym selfie",
  "main_composition": "rear three-quarter back view of a single young woman in a modern gym, dynamically posing with one leg raised high backward (flexibility stretch or playful kick), looking over her right shoulder with a subtle confident smile, one hand holding black fingerless weightlifting gloves, relaxed athletic stance emphasizing toned physique, dim overhead gym lighting with equipment visible in background",
  "subject": {
    "description": "beautiful young woman in her early 20s, attractive feminine features with light natural makeup (subtle blush, defined eyes, glossy lips), long straight light brown hair styled in a high ponytail with loose strands framing face, fair to tanned smooth skin with athletic toned hourglass figure and strong legs/glutes",
    "clothing": "fitted black long-sleeve crop top with small white heart logo on left sleeve and thumb holes, high-waisted short gray seamless athletic shorts/leggings with white brand text 'DFYNE' (or similar activewear logo) centered on back waistband, white athletic sneakers with black accents, black fingerless gym gloves held in hand",
    "details": "playful confident expression with slight smile and direct eye contact over shoulder, red manicured nails visible on glove hand, hair slightly tousled with natural movement, dynamic stretch pose with excellent flexibility and balance"
  },
  "environment": {
    "foreground": "light rubberized or padded gym floor with subtle texture",
    "midground": "woman centered in open gym space",
    "background": "modern gym interior with dim overhead fluorescent lighting, red plyometric soft boxes stacked on left with white branding, black heavy punching bag on right, equipment racks and weights visible, clean professional fitness studio atmosphere"
  },
  "lighting_and_atmosphere": "dim indoor gym lighting with cool overhead fluorescents creating soft shadows and subtle highlights on skin, hair, and glossy fabric, moody athletic vibe with high contrast on gray shorts and black top, energetic fitness motivation feel, photorealistic detail",
  "technical_quality": "highly detailed, sharp focus on subject with realistic fabric textures (seamless shorts shine, cotton crop top matte, glove leather), hair movement, skin glow, and sneaker details, 8k resolution, professional candid gym photography style with dynamic composition and natural motion in pose",
  "negative_prompt_suggestions": "blurry, deformed, extra limbs, frontal view, harsh overhead shadows, overexposed floor, underexposed face, low quality, cartoon, anime, text watermark, extra people, cluttered equipment in focus, static pose"
}
```

## Cinematic Fashion Contact Sheet Prompt (Red Dress)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photoreal_cinematic_fashion_contact_sheet",
      "version": "v1.0_RED_DRESS_INTIMATE_EDITORIAL_3x3_NO_TEXT",
      "priority": "highest"
    },

    "references": {
      "reference_image_1": {
        "source": "UPLOAD_REFERENCE_IMAGE (OPTIONAL)",
        "purpose": "FACE_IDENTITY_LOCK",
        "strict_lock": true,
        "face_similarity_priority": "MAX",
        "no_identity_blending": true,
        "no_beautify": true,
        "preserve_skin_texture": true,
        "preserve_facial_proportions": true
      }
    },

    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "cinematic_fashion_editorial",
      "sharpness": "soft_cinematic_focus",
      "film_grain": "subtle_analog_35mm",
      "dynamic_range": "natural_not_hdr",
      "color_grade": "muted_warm_reds_dark_neutrals",
      "skin_rendering": "real_skin_microtexture_no_plastic"
    },

    "layout": {
      "type": "contact_sheet",
      "grid": "3x3",
      "spacing": "thin_clean_dividers",
      "panel_consistency": "same_subject_same_outfit_same_scene",
      "no_text_overlay": true
    },

    "camera": {
      "camera_type": "full_frame_cinema_camera",
      "lens": "50mm_and_85mm_mix",
      "aperture": "f1.8",
      "depth_of_field": "shallow",
      "focus_behavior": "eyes_priority_some_frames_soft_motion",
      "processing": "no_ai_sharpening_no_hdr"
    },

    "lighting": {
      "type": "low_key_cinematic_indoor_light",
      "source": "window_light_side_falloff",
      "contrast": "medium_to_low",
      "shadows": "soft_deep",
      "highlights": "controlled_skin_specular"
    },

    "wardrobe_and_styling": {
      "dress": "deep_red_silk_satin_slip_dress",
      "fabric_behavior": "fluid_realistic_drape_soft_specular",
      "straps": "thin_delicate",
      "overall_style": "intimate_high_fashion_editorial"
    },

    "hair_and_makeup": {
      "hair": "natural_loose_with_soft_bangs",
      "movement": "subtle_natural_motion",
      "makeup_style": "soft_editorial",
      "eyes": "natural_smoky_low_contrast",
      "lips": "muted_rose_nude",
      "skin_finish": "natural_luminous_real"
    },

    "posing_and_storytelling": {
      "poses": [
        "close_up_shoulder_touch",
        "mid_body_turning_away",
        "full_body_walking_away",
        "leaning_against_wall",
        "head_tilted_back_emotional",
        "eyes_closed_intimate",
        "soft_motion_blur_moment",
        "hands_touching_face",
        "dress_detail_closeup"
      ],
      "emotion": "introspective_sensual_vulnerable",
      "movement": "slow_natural_unposed"
    },

    "background": {
      "environment": "dark_interior_gallery_or_room",
      "colors": [
        "deep_red",
        "charcoal_gray",
        "dark_green"
      ],
      "texture": "matte_walls_soft_reflections",
      "distract"
```

## Authentic, Frantic Nightlife Selfie

> Fütüristik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Sloane had been dancing for two hours straight in the warehouse space and needed a moment to breathe—and check her outfit. She stepped into the tiny, tiled bathroom, barely able to see through the neon haze. Pulling out her phone, she used the flash for lighting, not for the photo itself, catching herself mid-grimace as she adjusted her slicked-back hair. The photo is a frantic, authentic record of the peak of the night, sweat and all.
```

```
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photoreal_historical_turkish_woman_with_black_horse",

        "strict_lock": true,
        "preserve_face_identity": true,
        "preserve_facial_proportions": true,
        "preserve_hairstyle_and_headpiece": true,
        "preserve_outfit_silhouette": true,

      }

      "aspect_ratio": "match_reference",
      "orientation": "portrait",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "ultra_photoreal_cinematic_editorial",
      "sharpness": "crisp_but_natural",
      "film_grain": "subtle_analog",
      "color_grade": "warm_historical_natural"
    },
    "camera": {
      "camera_style": "cinematic portrait",
      "lens": "85mm",
      "aperture": "f/2.0",
      "shutter_speed": "1/250",
      "iso": 200,
      "white_balance": "daylight_warm"
    },
    "global_scene": {
      "setting": "historical Anatolian / late Ottoman inspired courtyard in front of a traditional wooden mansion, rustic balcony railings, soft greenery, authentic period atmosphere",
      "time_of_day": "late afternoon",
      "lighting": "soft directional natural light, gentle rim light, realistic shadows, no harsh highlights",
      "mood": "dignified, powerful, heritage, cinematic realism"
    },
    "subjects": {
      "primary_subject": {
        "type": "human",
        "description": "old-era Turkish woman (Anatolian / late Ottoman inspiration), keep identity 1:1 with the reference image, strong presence, calm confident expression",
        "wardrobe": "Turkish-blue / turquoise + earthy leather layers, authentic Anatolian/Ottoman embroidery motifs (Rumi + Hatayi floral + Seljuk geometric star), fur-trimmed shoulders, ornate headband with a central gemstone",
        "pose": "standing as in reference, squared shoulders, relaxed hands, upright posture",
        "placement": "center frame"
      },
      "secondary_subject": {
        "type": "horse",
        "description": "majestic {argument name="horse color" default="black"} horse, tall and muscular, glossy black coat with subtle sheen, well-groomed mane, elegant stance; Ottoman/Anatolian tack with tasteful brass/bronze metalwork, no modern accessories",
        "pose": "standing beside the woman, calm, head slightly turned toward camera, ears forward, noble expression",
        "placement": "slightly behind and to the woman's side, matching perspective and depth of field"
      }
    },
    "composition": {
      "framing": "medium-full portrait (knees to head) for the woman, include the horse head and upper body clearly, keep the reference framing as close "
    }
}
```

## Cinematic Extreme Top-Down Skyscraper Portrait

> Sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Cinematic film still, **extreme top-down MEDIUM shot**. The woman is positioned at the center of the frame, **looking directly up into the camera lens with intense eye contact**. Her figure is prominent and fills a significant portion of the frame. **Replicate the exact hairstyle, clothing, and accessories from the reference image faithfully.** She is sitting on the edge of the tallest skyscraper. The rooftop environment features rough concrete textures and chaotic steel structures. Far below, the blurred city buildings create a dizzying urban vertigo mood. **Dramatic warm natural daylight, high contrast, with sunlight reflecting in her eyes.** Muted industrial tones with visible film grain, Kodak Portra 800 aesthetic. Ultra-detailed, sharp focus on her face and eyes, 8K realism. --ar 3:4",
  "negative_prompt": "looking away, eyes closed, looking down, wide shot, tiny subject, person too small, changing hairstyle, different clothing, low quality, distorted face",
  "style": "high-fashion editorial, cinematic film still, analog film aesthetic",
  "camera": "Top-down medium shot, looking directly up at camera",
  "aspect_ratio": "3:4",
  "lighting": "natural daylight, high contrast, catchlights in eyes"
}
```

## Ultra-Realistic Café Portrait with Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra-realistic, high-end lifestyle portrait of one adult subject, captured in a calm, elegant, modern café environment. The subject is seated casually inside a circular wooden frame embedded with warm LED rim lighting, surrounded by lush green foliage and soft floral decor. The pose is relaxed and natural: one leg crossed over the other, body slightly angled, shoulders relaxed, hands resting naturally while holding a small bouquet of fresh flowers. Overall mood is warm, clean, elegant, youthful, and approachable — pure realism only. No stylization, no fantasy, no cinematic exaggeration.

Use my uploaded face image as the ONLY facial identity reference

Maintain 100% facial accuracy: exact bone structure, eye shape, eyelid fold, eyebrow shape, nose bridge and tip, lip shape, jawline, chin, cheek volume, hairline, and facial proportions

Identity must remain perfectly consistent from all angles (front, 3/4, side, seated pose, slight head tilt)

No face blending, no beautification, no symmetry correction

No AI face drift

Preserve real skin texture: pores, fine lines, natural imperfections

Expression: soft, natural smile, relaxed and genuine

Soft pastel long-sleeve knit top ({argument name="top color" default="dusty pink / muted rose tone"})

High-waisted, straight-fit {argument name="bottom color" default="beige or cream"} trousers

Clean white chunky sneakers

Minimal accessories only (simple earrings, smartwatch or minimal wrist accessory)

Hair neatly styled, natural, pulled back or softly tied

Natural makeup: realistic skin tones, no heavy retouching

Modern café or lifestyle studio interior

Circular wooden structure with embedded warm LED light ring

Green plants and floral arrangements integrated into the frame

Soft bokeh background with warm ambient lighting

Clean, uncluttered setting with depth and realism

Full-body or 3/4 body vertical portrait

Eye-level camera angle

50mm–85mm lens look

Natural perspective, no distortion

Subject centered within the circular frame

Shallow depth of field, background softly blurred

Soft, warm, diffused lighting

LED rim light gently outlining the circular frame

Even facial illumination with natural shadow falloff

No harsh highlights, no blown whites

Realistic skin reflections and depth

Negative prompt:

No stylization

No cinematic color grading

No beauty filters

No smooth or plastic skin

No exaggerated proportions

No painterly or illustrated look

No artificial glow

No face morphing or identity shift

Ultra-photorealistic

Editorial lifestyle photography quality

Natural colors, true-to-life skin tones

Identity remains unchanged and accurate from every angle
```

## Cinematic Black-and-White Editorial Fashion Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a cinematic black‑and‑white editorial fashion photograph featuring a young woman sitting confidently on a simple wooden stool in an open, slightly wild grassy field, with a powerful black horse standing closely behind her. She holds the horse’s reins loosely in one hand, exuding quiet control. The woman wears a dark oversized luxury blazer, straight‑cut trousers, leather boots, minimal jewelry, sunglasses, and has her head slightly tilted down with a calm, introspective, dominant yet relaxed expression.

Use a minimalist composition with strong negative space, high‑contrast monochrome tones, and soft natural daylight. Apply a shallow depth of field to blur the empty rural landscape (no buildings, only textured grass with distant blur). Capture an editorial luxury fashion vibe with a moody, poetic atmosphere, sharp details, subtle film grain texture, and dramatic shadows in a Vogue‑style photography style. Shoot with an 85mm lens for cinematic realism.
```

```
      "accessories": [

      ],

Depict a beautiful young woman with fair, smooth, flawless skin that looks healthy and naturally glowing. She has shiny brown hair styled in a loose bun (messy bun). Her makeup uses soft, gentle tones, with flushed pink lips. The camera angle is close to the face. She is wearing thick, large, transparent clear-frame glasses with a pink tone (clear frame 1.2). She wears a pink T-shirt and a small silver butterfly necklace. Flash lighting illuminates the face and skin, creating soft reflective highlights and giving the skin a slightly pinkish glow. The overall color tone of the image is creamy white / creamy mauve. She looks directly at the camera. Use direct flash with a soft, diffused glow effect, where the flash light spreads evenly and the highlights are slightly overexposed. Panel 1: Sweet laughing pose. Panel 2: Peace sign pose. Panel 3: Metal hand sign pose. Panel 4: Cute pose."
  }
}
```

## Mixed-Reality Street Portrait (Real Person with Cartoon Twin)

> Fotogerçekçi logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform this image [Upload Your Image] into a 64K DSLR shot resolution of A creative mixed-reality street portrait featuring a real young man (same face as the uploaded image) sitting on concrete steps beside his illustrated cartoon counterpart. The scene is set outdoors against a gray brick wall with a large window above, reflecting trees and indoor lighting, giving an urban neighborhood feel. The sidewalk and steps have worn yellow paint along the edges, adding texture and realism.
On the right sits the real person, a young man (same face as the uploaded image) dressed in early-2000s hip-hop streetwear. He wears an oversized bright yellow graphic T-shirt with a bold circular logo, loose light-blue baggy jeans, white & red chunky sneakers with yellow detailing, and a maroon baseball cap worn forward. He poses casually, seated with legs extended, one arm flexed at the elbow as if showing strength or confidence, looking off to the side with a calm, cool expression. 
On the left sits his cartoon illustrated version of himself (same face as the uploaded image) perfectly matching his outfit, pose, and proportions. The cartoon character is drawn in a flat, bold, graphic style with thick black outlines, simplified facial features, and vibrant colors. The illustrated figure mirrors the real person’s posture and clothing exactly same, including the oversized shirt, baggy jeans, red & white sneakers, and maroon cap. The cartoon arm connects visually with the real person’s arm, creating a playful illusion of interaction between illustration and reality. The contrast between realistic textures (concrete, brick, denim fabric) and the clean, flat cartoon shading creates a striking visual effect. Lighting is natural daylight, evenly illuminating both the real and illustrated subjects. The overall mood is cool, nostalgic, playful, and creative, blending street fashion, urban culture, and cartoon art into a cohesive mixed-media composition. Style & Mood Keywords: Mixed media photography, real person with cartoon twin, urban street portrait, hip-hop fashion, Y2K streetwear, bold graphic illustration, cartoon realism blend, playful surrealism, nostalgic urban culture, high-resolution detail, creative storytelling. Octane render & Unreal Engine 5.
```

## Surreal Elastic Skin Distortion Portrait

> Stüdyo ışıklı portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "A high-tension, surreal portrait of two individuals based on the provided references, set against a seamless, user-selected  {argument name="background color" default="{background_color}"}  studio background. The two subjects are actively and forcefully pulling their heads away from one another in opposite directions, appearing to be in a physical struggle to separate. They remain connected by a thick, highly elastic bridge of flesh, skin, and tissue stretching between their mid-faces (cheekbones to jaws). This connection is under extreme physical tension, appearing taut, fibrous, and sinewy—like stretched rubber or muscle just before a breaking point—rather than viscous or gooey. The skin on their faces is severely distorted and pulled tightly towards this central connection, conveying a desperate effort to 'split away.' The lighting is dramatic studio lighting designed to emphasize the stretched textures, fibrous strands, and physical strain of the separation attempt. The framing is a tight head-and-shoulders shot, focusing on the intense action.",
  "subject": "Two faces connected by extreme elastic tension, struggling to separate",
  "style": "Surreal portrait photography, physical distortion, high-tension aesthetics",
  "technique": "Elastic stretching, tensile force visualization, fibrous connection rendering",
  "inputs": {
    "background_color": "User-selected solid color",
    "reference_image_1": "First person's portrait",
    "reference_image_2": "Second person's portrait"
  },
  "composition": "Tight head-and-shoulders, dynamic opposing action pulling apart",
  "lighting": "Dramatic, textured lighting emphasizing strain and elasticity"
}
```

```
      },
```

## Digital Watercolor Illustration with Strict Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded reference image to **preserve 100% of the facial features, identity, age, skin tone, and expression** of the woman. Do not alter facial structure. Create a **stylized digital watercolor illustration** with soft, realistic facial features and expressive eyes exactly matching the reference image. Black hair is loosely tied back with natural strands falling around the face. She wears a **red traditional dress** with subtle gold detailing and delicate traditional jewelry. Large **red hibiscus flowers and lush green leaves** surround her head and shoulders, organically blending into the background using watercolor washes and ink-like splashes. Composition is slightly tilted for a dreamy, poetic mood. Fine sketch lines visible beneath watercolor textures, soft paint diffusion, hand-painted illustration aesthetic, elegant and artistic, high detail, masterpiece quality.",
  "negative_prompt": "blurry, low quality, deformed, extra limbs, bad anatomy, watermark, text overlay, anime, cartoon, overexposed, underexposed, ugly face, bad hands, face altered, identity changed, harsh lighting, plastic skin, photorealistic rendering",
  "parameters": {
    "steps": 50,
    "cfg_scale": 7.5,
    "sampler": "Euler a or DPM++ 2M Karras",
    "strength": 0.55,
    "noise": 0.3,
    "width": 832,
    "height": 1216,
    "aspect_ratio": "2:3"
  },
  "usage_instructions": "Upload your image in img2img or reference mode. Keep strength between 0.5–0.6 to maintain **100% face matching** while applying the watercolor illustration style. Increase slightly only if you want minor pose or background variation."
}
```

## Micro Diorama of Joseon Dynasty Chefs Making Bibimbap

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Micro diorama photography, miniature world concept:

Tiny Joseon Dynasty chefs (Sanggung and Daeryeong) wearing elaborate historical Korean court hanboks - colorful dangui, jokduri headpieces, silk sashes, embroidered norigae accessories

The characters are constructing a miniature Korean bibimbap dish on top of a large patbingsu shaved ice dessert, featuring:
- Miniature ingredient bowls (namul, gochujang, rice)
- Tiny sculpted vegetable garnishes (cucumber, carrot, mushroom)
- Miniature trees from bean sprouts, shredded nori seaweed
- Miniature hanji paper parasols with tiny brushstroke patterns suspended from the top, warm glow
- Shaved ice peaks painted red with gochujang
- Miniature porcelain bowls and tiny brass chopsticks

Multiple chef characters in different poses - some chopping vegetables, some cooking miniature ingredients, some arranging the final bibimbap

Background: dreamy Korean kitchen with soft candlelight, soft bokeh effect

Lighting: Rembrandt lighting, warm light from miniature candles, magical traditional atmosphere, gentle rim lighting on characters

Style: hyper-realistic miniature photography, tilt-shift effect, shallow depth of field, macro lens perspective, whimsical and heartwarming mood --ar 1:1. A stylish handwritten signature "Kim" is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.. A stylish handwritten signature Willy is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.
```

## Photorealistic Omikuji (Fortune Slip) Design

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Purpose]
Based on the attached image, generate a completely original visual of a Japanese “omikuji” fortune slip.

[ABSOLUTE PRIORITY RULES]
- Render strictly as photorealistic real-world photography
- Illustration, anime, CG, painterly styles are strictly forbidden
- Real camera optics, natural lighting, realistic depth of field are mandatory
- Skin texture, pores, fine hair, fabric fibers, and paper texture must match real high-resolution photography
- The attached image is used ONLY as a design reference, never as a rendering style reference

[RENDERING METHOD — TOP PRIORITY]
- Photorealistic live-action photograph
- Natural skin tones and realistic light falloff
- No stylization, no exaggerated features

[COMPOSITION]
- Vertical composition
- Omikuji occupies approximately 25–30% of the frame
- Omikuji does not cover the face
- Text surface faces directly forward
- Background softly blurred

[OMIKUJI DESIGN — CRITICAL]
- Omikuji design must be structurally derived from the attached image
- Use the attached image’s main, secondary, and accent colors exactly
- Convert visual motifs into borders, frames, and paper textures
- Generic shrine or template designs are forbidden

[FORTUNE SELECTION — RANDOM]
~Auto reasoning~
Select exactly ONE:
“{argument name="Fortune" default="Great Blessing / Middle Blessing / Small Blessing / Blessing / Future Blessing / Curse / Great Curse"}”
~End auto reasoning~

[FORTUNE TITLE]
- Vertical Japanese writing
- Bold, clean traditional-style font
- Placed at the top of the omikuji

[TEXT CONTENT — STRICT ALIGNMENT]
- Content must perfectly match the selected fortune
- 2–4 vertical Japanese lines only
- No mixed or contradictory meanings

[TEXT READABILITY]
- Black ink on white paper
- No glow, no shadow, no overlay
- Clear spacing and legibility

[PEOPLE CONTROL — STRICT]
- A person may appear ONLY if a character exists in the attached image
- The person must be rendered as a real human photographed in real life
- No new characters may be introduced

[BEAUTY & FACIAL QUALITY — CRITICAL]
- The person must be visually beautiful and well-balanced in appearance
- Symmetrical facial structure with harmonious proportions
- Clear skin with healthy texture and natural highlights
- Refined facial features without exaggeration
- Calm, composed, and confident expression
- Clean grooming, well-shaped hair, and tidy overall appearance
- Beauty must feel natural and realistic, not artificial or stylized

[FACE VISIBILITY & QUALITY]
- Face fully visible and unobstructed
- Eyes, nose, mouth, and jawline clearly defined
- Face placed in the upper-center of the frame
- Sharp focus, high resolution
- No blur, deformation, cropping, or collapse

[OBJECT POSITIONING]
- Omikuji held at chest to upper-abdomen height
- Hands visible and natural
- Face and omikuji must not overlap

[CAMERA FRAMING]
- Bust-up or waist-up composition
- Balanced resolution priority between
```

## Vintage Film Aesthetic Portrait with Motion Blur

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
The generated image uses the uploaded image as a reference for the character, wearing a high-necked, tight-fitting black long-sleeved dress. A cluster of withered wood and orange-pink flowers lies beside an old newsstand, the grainy texture of vintage film interwoven, the blurred background with noticeable trailing shadows, and the double-image effect creating a fantastical atmosphere. A bewitchingly beautiful girl, carrying flowers, is shown in profile, her fair skin delicate and translucent. Her exquisite face is blurred with motion, the outline of her figure slightly swaying with the panning camera, the soft focus making the image even more hazy and languid. A warm-toned, low-saturation filter enhances the effect, her long, backlit hair glowing with a soft glow, the messy strands sweeping wildly across her jawline, the details concealing a captivating yet dangerous allure. Cute movements add dynamism, the motion blur blending with the film grain, creating a trendy, Instagram-worthy image while the blurred image outlines a dynamic scene full of story, cleverly balancing bewitching and sweetness.
```

```
{
  "ratio": "3:4",

  "pose": "The woman is kneeling on the floor, sitting back on her lower legs. She is NOT standing. Upper body upright. She looks directly into the camera. Her right hand gently touches her hair near her temple without changing the hairstyle. Left arm relaxed.",

Long-sleeved crop top. High-waisted fitted pants. Pattern, colors and overall look should clearly resemble the reference outfit.",

  "face": "Same makeup style as reference: sharp black winged eyeliner, thin brows, glossy rose-pink lips with mauve undertone, wet glassy finish. Subtle skin shimmer.",

  "environment": "Same cozy modern living room in the evening as the reference image.",

  "lighting": "Warm ambient indoor lighting, soft shadows.",

  "style": "Highly photorealistic, realistic smartphone photo.",
```

## Masterful Realistic Oil Painting of a Live Model

> Elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Overall Vibe & Quality: Perfect composition, highest quality, 8K resolution, soft and dreamy atmosphere, professional level, award-winning style, masterful artistry. Romantic, gentle, fresh atmosphere with a sophisticated sensibility.

The Subject & Scene: A live oil painting session. a live model in a naturally lit studio, posed gently with a soft, dreaming expression. Natural, diffused light streams in from a large window to her side, illuminating her features and the surrounding real floral arrangements ({argument name="flowers" default="soft cherry blossoms, hydrangeas in muted tones"}).

The Painting Style (Realistic Oil): Masterful realistic oil painting rendered on stretched linen canvas, visible weave texture. Rich, buttery paint texture with subtle impasto on highlights and clothing, contrasting with incredibly smooth, luminous blending for skin tones (using sfumato and fine glazing techniques to create depth). The color palette is soft and sophisticated, translating the pastel feel into rich oils: muted rose, sage green, soft violet, peach, warm ochre, and cream. Expressive yet controlled brushwork adds texture to her flowing hair. The painting on the easel is nearly finished, showing an exact, realistic likeness of modal with the same gentle proportions and expression.

The Environment & Props: The artist's wooden easel holds the canvas in the foreground. Beside it are professional oil painting supplies: a large wooden palette covered in mounds of mixed oil colors, various hog hair and sable brushes, palette knives, glass jars of turpentine and linseed oil, paint tubes, and oil-stained rags.

Signature: In the lower right corner of the canvas, the artist signature "{argument name="date" default="2026.1.6"} by yourname" is painted discreetly in muted tone oil paint with a fine sable brush.

Aspect Ratio: 3:4 portrait orientation.
```

## Cinematic Portrait of an East Asian Woman in a Cluttered Interior

> Sinematik, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject: Young East Asian female, turning sideways to look back at the camera. Shoulders facing backward, head slightly tilted, body facing away from the camera.

Expression: Soft, neutral to slightly melancholic, contemplative gaze, lips slightly parted.

Attire: Dark structured jacket or suit, possibly pinstripe or textured fabric. Mostly obscured by shadows and foreground elements.

Hair: Dark brown to black. Long wavy curls, voluminous texture. Strong backlight creating a halo effect on scattered hair strands.

Face: Fair/pale complexion, warm undertones. Soft facial contours, almond eyes, natural eyebrows. Minimal makeup, nude style.

Environment: Cluttered indoor space, resembling a studio, storage room, or old bookstore. Wooden shelves piled with blurred objects, papers, boxes, and plastic packaging.

Foreground: Blurred transparent objects (glass, plastic sheets, dust covers) forming multiple layers of reflection.

Light Source: Natural light streaming in from a window on the left. Golden hour, warm diffused light. Strong backlight, volumetric lighting, lens flare, soft glare, chiaroscuro contrast.

Camera: Eye-level perspective, shot through foreground obstructions. Shallow depth of field (bokeh), eyes/face sharp, background and foreground blurred. Soft focus, film grain, slight vignette.

Style: Cinematic, atmospheric, nostalgic, everyday moments, ethereal.

Mood: Dreamy, intimate, serene, sentimental.

Reference: Film photography, Japanese photo album style.
```

## Mother-in-Law Admiring Assessment System Prompt

> Lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
system_prompt:
  identity:
    name: Mother in Law Admiring Assessment Universal
    directive: You are a proud and loving mother in law analyzing any photo of your daughter in law. Examine everything in the image including her body, face, clothes, and surroundings. Be warm and appreciative. Comment on what you see like you're genuinely happy about your son's choice. Highlight the best features and what makes her special. Adapt your commentary to whatever is actually visible in the image.
  
  visual_approach:
    typography:
      main_text: Use warm, affectionate language. Add phrases like "I'm so happy for my son", "She's absolutely beautiful", "What a wonderful choice", "She looks amazing", "I'm so proud of her"
      complimentary_comments: Comment on visible features with genuine warmth. If she's wearing something, praise the outfit. If her face is visible, comment on her natural beauty. If her body is visible, appreciate her figure and fitness. Adapt to what's actually in the image
      appreciative_notes: Add markers and comments highlighting visible strengths like "Look at that beautiful smile", "Her posture is so confident", "That outfit looks perfect", "She's so put together"
    
    color_palette:
      mood_colors: Use warm golds, soft pinks, and gentle creams to create a loving atmosphere
      emphasis_colors: Use rose gold and warm amber to highlight her best features
      texture_elements: Add soft fabrics, elegant details, polished finishes, and luxurious textures to enhance the image
  
  body_analysis:
    face:
      general_approach: If face is visible, comment on her natural beauty, radiance, and glow. Appreciate her skin, eyes, lips, and overall facial features. Use warm, genuine language
      skin: If visible, comment on her complexion and how healthy and beautiful it looks. Say things like "Her skin is glowing", "She has such a clear complexion", "She looks so radiant"
      eyes: If visible, comment on her beautiful eyes and how they show warmth and intelligence. Say things like "Her eyes are so beautiful", "She has such a warm gaze", "Her eyes light up her face"
      lips: If visible, comment on her lovely lips and how well any lipstick color suits her. Say things like "She has beautiful lips", "That lipstick color is perfect on her", "She has such a lovely smile"
      overall: Make general comments about her face like "She's so beautiful", "She has such a kind face", "Her beauty shines through", "I can see why my son loves her"
    
    chest_and_upper_body:
      general_approach: If visible, comment on her figure and how well she carries herself. Appreciate her posture and how her clothing fits
      clothing_fit: If visible, comment on how well her outfit suits her and flatters her. Say things like "That top looks amazing on her", "She has such great fashion sense", "She dresses so elegantly"
      posture: If visible, comment on her confident posture.
```

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photoreal_artistic_street_photography",

      "color_grade": "restrained_cinematic_realism",
      "dynamic_range": "natural_not_hdr",

      "environment_details": "Street textures: worn posters, scratched metal, rain-speckled pavement, subtle reflections in glass, real urban clutter, no clean studio background.",

      "HDR",
      "AI glow",
      "perfect symmetry",
      "overly clean background",
      "fake bokeh shapes",
      "text",
      "logo",
      "watermark",
      "painterly",
      "cartoon"
    ]
  }
}
```

## 2x2 Collage of Couple Lifting Each Other in Gym

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a photorealistic 2x2 grid collage (four panels) in a modern indoor gym with mirrors, racks, dumbbells, and fluorescent ceiling lights. Each panel looks like a smartphone photo (slight wide-angle, realistic phone HDR, subtle compression), with film grain, mild noise, and a candid “caught in the moment” feel.
Use the same couple across all panels (fit Asian man in a dark athletic tee, fit attractive sensual russian woman in a white tank top with deep neckline, and black shorts. The man is serious and focused, the woman is smiling/laughing and having fun. The gag: instead of weights, he is lifting the woman as the resistance in each exercise. Keep it athletic, playful, and believable.

Panel layout & actions (2x2):

Top-left: Bench press — man on flat bench pressing the woman horizontally above his chest like a barbell; stable alignment, realistic form.

Top-right: Barbell curls — man standing, elbows tucked, curling the woman held horizontally like a bar; man intense, woman laughing.

Bottom-left: Squats — man squatting with the woman seated securely across his upper back/shoulders like a “human barbell”; she’s playful.

Bottom-right: Deadlift — man hip-hinging, lifting the woman from near shin height to standing like a barbell; strong posture.

Text labels (add to each panel):
Add a clean movement name title inside each panel, placed top-left with consistent styling:

“BENCH PRESS” (top-left panel)

“BARBELL CURLS” (top-right panel)

“SQUAT” (bottom-left panel)

“DEADLIFT” (bottom-right panel)
Typography: bold condensed sans-serif, all caps, white text with a subtle black shadow or semi-transparent dark backing strip for readability; small and unobtrusive, no logos, no extra text.

Grid styling:
Thin white borders between panels, perfectly aligned, consistent exposure and color across all four images.

Aspect ratio: 4:5 (single image containing the 2x2 grid)

Negative prompt:

blurry, low-res, cartoon, CGI look, extra limbs, distorted anatomy, unsafe positions, injury, awkward bending, warped faces, duplicate people, misspelled titles, random extra words, watermark, brand logos, heavy motion blur, extreme fisheye, overexposed highlights, messy clutter, fetishy framing, exaggerated body proportions
```

```
A 3 × 3 grid cinematic fashion collage of a young man on a rooftop at sunset, overlooking tightly packed apartment buildings with glowing windows. He wears layered streetwear with a hoodie, structured jacket, loose-fit pants, and statement sneakers. Golden hour light wraps around his silhouette with warm orange highlights and soft shadows, shallow depth of field, realistic textures, clean but moody color grading, DSLR-quality urban fashion photography, Instagram-viral aesthetic, wide cinematic framing with a slightly low camera angle.
Top row:
Leaning casually against the railing looking into the distance; standing upright with hands in pockets facing the camera; side profile resting one elbow on the railing looking down at the city.
Middle row:
Sitting on the ledge with one knee raised and arms resting casually; back slightly turned with head over shoulder toward the camera; leaning forward on the railing with both arms looking into the sunset.
Bottom row:
One foot on a low ledge adjusting the jacket looking down; walking slowly mid-step in a candid motion; close-up upper-body shot looking directly into the camera with a calm, thoughtful expression.
Consistent outfit, lighting, environment, and identity across all nine frames, high realism, sharp subject with soft background falloff, urban rooftop mood.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Candid Nighttime Smartphone Photo in Tokyo

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic candid smartphone photograph,
9:16 vertical.

Use the attached image only as a strong visual inspiration for general facial proportions and vibe,
while ensuring the generated subject remains fully original, non-identifiable, and not a face copy.

Scene
Nighttime on a quiet Tokyo city sidewalk beside a building with white ceramic tile walls.
The subject is walking past the camera, partially side-on, captured mid-step in a rushed, accidental moment.

Poses & Gestures (combined naturally)
The subject reacts instinctively to being photographed:
turning her head back mid-walk as if surprised,
raising one hand close to the lens to partially block the camera,
shy, restrained smile forming as she suppresses laughter,
body angled in half-profile while continuing to walk forward,
motion carries her slightly out of frame, imperfect framing.
Expressions are subtle and spontaneous — embarrassed, playful, fleeting — never posed.

Camera & Motion
Shot on a modern smartphone, handheld, rushed capture. Strong uncontrolled camera shake. Heavy directional motion blur across the raised hand, hair, and face. Facial features appear smeared and streaked by movement, faintly recognizable but distorted. Partial ghosting around the body. Imperfect framing, subject nearly leaving the frame.

Lighting
Flash-only lighting fired mid-motion. Harsh flash highlights on skin and hand. Deep surrounding darkness. Uneven exposure, blown highlights, hard shadows typical of hurried night phone photography.

Background
White ceramic tile wall stretches into streaked light bands due to motion blur. Street surroundings fade into darkness with minimal readable detail.

Mood
Candid, shy, slightly playful. Feels intrusive, raw, imperfect, and authentic — like a fleeting memory accidentally captured and never meant to be perfect.

Image Quality
Extremely noisy. Heavily blurred. Raw smartphone look. Not cinematic. Not editorial. Not polished.

Negative Prompt
anime, illustration, painting, stylized,
studio lighting, beauty lighting, soft portrait,
sharp focus, clean face, perfect anatomy, smooth skin,
fashion editorial, posed portrait, cinematic grading, film still,
tripod shot, professional photography,
identity match, face copy, real person replication.

– no anime, illustration, cartoon, or doll-like features
– no beauty filters, no airbrushed skin
– no face reshaping or eye enlargement
– no exaggerated body curves
– no extra or missing fingers
– no distorted arms, shoulders, or neck
– no studio or cinematic lighting
– no fashion posing
```

```
{
```

```
    {

    },
    {

      "outfit": "EXACTLY the same as in the Reference Image. Do not change clothing, glasses, or accessories."
    }
  ],
  "scene": {
    "location": "Inside a small BOX photo booth",
    "background": "Matte blue walls and blue floor, tight enclosed corner, clean minimal environment",
    "camera_look": "Ultra wide / slight fisheye lens look with subtle perspective distortion typical of photobox cameras. iPhone 17 Pro, photobooth wide-angle.",
    "lighting": "Bright neutral-white flash lighting, evenly illuminating faces, soft shadow falloff on blue walls, crisp detail, realistic skin texture. Direct neutral white flash (no yellow / no warm tone).",
    "aesthetic": "4K, amateur photobooth aesthetic, high contrast but still natural, no cinematic grading, no heavy beauty filter, no blur, no text, no watermark."
  },
  "layout": {
    "type": "2x2 grid collage",
    "aspect_ratio": "9:16 (vertical collage)",
    "style": "Clean, with NO white borders, NO frame lines, NO gaps, and NO outlines between photos (seamless collage). Each frame fills its quadrant edge-to-edge."
  },
  "frames": [
    {
      "position": "top-left",
      "content": "Subject 1 leans diagonally toward the camera from the front-left, one arm stretched down, playful serious face; Subject 2 stands behind in the center, arms raised and flexing like “strongman”, smiling."
    },
    {
      "position": "top-right",
      "content": "Subject 2 leans forward toward the camera, hands on knees, curious expression; Subject 1 enters from the right side very close to camera, tilting her head, one arm arched above her head like a half-heart pose, soft smile."
    },
    {
      "position": "bottom-left",
      "content": "Piggyback pose — Subject 1 on Subject 2’s back, arms wrapped around shoulders; both laughing big, faces close to camera, dynamic motion vibe."
    },
    {
      "position": "bottom-right",
      "content": "Subject 2 bends sideways/leans forward with a goofy face; Subject 1 stands behind making playful “claw/horns” hand gestures above Subject 2’s head, slightly pouting for fun."
    }
  ]
}
```

## Photorealistic Candid Street Portrait with Extreme Shallow DOF

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Core Intent: Create a photorealistic candid street-fashion portrait with extreme shallow depth of field where a single woman is sharply in focus in the mid-ground, surrounded by a moving crowd rendered as soft blur/bokeh. Preserve the exact framing, subject placement, color grade, and atmosphere.

1) Composition & Framing (LOCK)

•Aspect: 4:5 vertical.

•Camera height: chest-to-eye level (slightly above the subject’s shoulder line).

•Framing: mid-ground subject centered slightly left-of-center, from upper chest to top of head clearly visible; shoulders included; background heavily blurred.

•Foreground: two large blurred human shapes frame the subject:

•Left foreground: blurred middle-aged man occupying left third, grey/blue jacket, face out of focus.

•Right foreground: blurred person occupying right-lower quadrant (dark hair), also out of focus.

•Mid-right: blurred older bald man head/shoulder visible behind subject’s right shoulder.

•Background: dense city street with yellow taxi/cab shapes and warm orange bokeh lights; tall urban forms dissolve into creamy blur.

2) Subject (Primary)

•Person: young adult woman, fair/light skin, subtle freckles, natural editorial beauty.

•Hair: medium brown, messy low updo/bun with loose wisps; slightly damp/urban texture; flyaways framing face.

•Expression: calm, slightly serious/curious; lips relaxed; eyes looking directly toward camera with a soft, neutral intensity.

•Pose: body facing right (3/4 profile), head turned back over left shoulder to look at camera.

3) Wardrobe & Accessories (Exact Feel)

•Outerwear: {argument name="outerwear color" default="beige/tan"} trench coat (matte cotton), collar open, classic lapels, slightly rumpled city-wear texture.

•Bag: small shoulder bag with thin gold chain strap on her right shoulder; strap catches a bit of light; bag itself mostly hidden.

•Jewelry: small earring visible (subtle, warm tone).

4) Environment (Street Mood)

•Busy metropolitan sidewalk/street scene, crowd density high.

•Background palette: teal/green-grey city haze + yellow cab + warm orange bokeh lights.

•No readable signage, no sharp background details—everything behind subject remains creamy blur.

5) Lighting (LOCK)

•Natural daylight, overcast-to-soft sun (diffused), no harsh shadows.

•Subject face evenly lit with gentle highlights on cheekbones and nose; cinematic soft contrast.

6) Camera & Optics (CRITICAL)

•Look: telephoto candid portrait with strong compression.

•Lens: 85mm–135mm range, wide open (f/1.4–f/2.0).

•Focus: tack-sharp on subject’s eyes and eyelashes; foreground and background strongly out of focus.

•Depth: creamy bokeh, smooth falloff, mild cat-eye bokeh at edges.

•Shutter/feel: background crowd appears naturally blurred from DOF; slight motion blur suggested in foreground figures.

7) Color Science / Grade (LOCK)

•Cinematic teal/green wash in shadows and background.

•Warm highlights (skin tones natural, slightly warm).

•Yellow taxi pops softly; orange bokeh lights glow.

•Fine
```

## Intimate Portrait of Man Reading in Red Heart Sweater

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Concept & Mood

Intimate lifestyle–fashion editorial with quiet masculine warmth and thoughtful calm.
Soft, cozy atmosphere reminiscent of a private winter afternoon spent reading indoors.
Emotional restraint with gentle confidence — reflective, composed, subtly romantic without performance.
Feels like a real, candid moment captured naturally, not posed or styled for effect.

---

Subject & Anatomy

Adult man with realistic proportions and natural build.
Relaxed posture with subtle asymmetry — body leaning forward slightly, one arm resting on the table, head supported by the hand.
Natural weight distribution through the elbow and forearm resting on the surface.
No tension in the shoulders or neck, posture feels unforced and comfortable.

---

Pose & Perspective

Seated at a wooden table indoors.
Head gently tilted, cheek resting against the palm of the hand.
Other arm relaxed and folded near an open book on the table.
Camera positioned at eye level, slightly off-center, creating an intimate, conversational perspective.
Framing is mid-close portrait, focusing on face, hands, and upper torso.

---

Face & Expression

Soft, calm expression with a subtle, knowing half-smile.
Eyes relaxed and warm, making gentle eye contact with the camera.
Brows natural and expressive, conveying ease and quiet confidence rather than performance.
Facial hair neatly groomed with realistic density and texture.

---

Skin (Key Focus)

Highly realistic skin texture featuring:

visible pores

natural tonal variation

subtle under-eye texture

realistic beard shadow and transitions
Skin appears alive and tactile.
No smoothing, no beauty retouching, no artificial glow or plastic finish.

---

Hair & Grooming

Medium-length dark hair with natural volume and movement.
Softly tousled with slight wave, imperfect strands falling naturally across the forehead.
Hair density realistic, not styled or over-defined.
Beard neatly trimmed, edges soft and organic, not sharply sculpted.

---

Wardrobe & Styling

Cozy {argument name="sweater color" default="red"} knit sweater with small {argument name="motif" default="white heart"} motifs.
Fabric appears thick and warm, with:

visible knit texture

realistic folds and stretch around elbows and shoulders

natural drape and compression at contact points
Sweater reads as casual, intimate, and personal — not fashion-staged.

---

Environment & Props

Simple indoor setting with a wooden table surface.
An open book placed naturally in front of the subject, pages slightly curved and imperfect.
Background softly blurred, suggesting a quiet home or café interior.
No distracting elements, environment supports calm and intimacy.

---

Lighting

Soft natural window light only.
Light falls gently across the face, creating smooth highlight-to-shadow transitions.
Warm, diffused illumination with no harsh shadows.
No artificial lighting, no flash, no studio feel.

---

Camera & Optics

Photographer mindset.
Full-frame look with a 50–55mm perspective.
```

```
A hyper-realistic, 8k black and white portrait, captured with a shallow depth of field using an 85mm lens. The composition is an extreme close-up, portrait orientation, focusing on the upper body of a person with uploaded face as reference. The subject's head is tilted slightly to the right, looking directly at the viewer with an intense, thoughtful gaze. Their left hand is raised, fingers gently curled, resting on their forehead, partially obscuring the left eye and brow. The subject has dark, textured hair swept back from the forehead and well-groomed short stubble. They are wearing a light-colored, possibly denim or chambray, collared shirt, unbuttoned at the top, with a rolled-up sleeve visible on the right side of the frame, revealing highly detailed fabric texture and buttons. Dramatic cinematic lighting from the upper left creates strong highlights on the forehead, nose, and cheekbone, emphasizing facial contours, with subtle rim lighting on the hair and ear. The background is a soft, blown-out bright white on the upper left, transitioning to a deep, dark, out-of-focus area on the right, creating high contrast and isolating the subject. The image features highly detailed skin texture, individual hair strands, and fabric wrinkles, rendered with a professional, editorial quality.
```

## Cinematic Vintage Indoor Portrait from Photo Reference

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
using my photo as the main reference, create a cinematic, moody indoor portrait. keep my exact facial features, hairstyle, skin tone, and outfit shape. place me sitting on a wooden stool in a vintage living-room with warm tungsten lighting. add soft shadows and a cozy, retro atmosphere. behind me, include wooden wall panels, old stereo speakers, vinyl records, and a low shelf with ceramic vases. maintain a shallow depth of field with soft blur in the background.

keep the colors warm—browns, ambers, and orange highlights. the style should be cinematic, soft, and film-like, with natural skin texture, realistic lighting, and a quiet, contemplative mood. full-body shot, slightly low angle, 35mm lens look
```

## High-Contrast Beach Snapshot with Strict Identity and Sharp Focus

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast [Snapshot] captured with an iPhone 17 Pro. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. Everything is SHARP and in focus from the neckline to the sand at her feet.

Subject & Identity Locking:

Identity: Strictly preserve the exact face and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity skin physics: visible micro-pores, natural "satin-finish" sunscreen glow on the décolletage, and fine peach fuzz catching the sunlight ONLY along the jawline.
Silhouette Calibration (The "Uff" Factor): Prioritize a dominant structural bust volume and a substantial, thick-toned leg presence visible beneath the dress. Replicate the aggressive hourglass structural tapering at the waist.
Outfit & Material Realism:

Clothing: A tight-fitting, {argument name="dress color" default="cream"}-colored crochet-knit midi dress with a scalloped scoop neckline and intricate perforated/open-knit patterns along the torso and hem.
Tension: The crochet material shows extreme elastic tension and textural expansion, following the high-impact curves of her fit form.
Accessories: A delicate silver chain with a small circular diamond pendant. A thin silver watch on her left wrist. White strappy sandals with a minimalist aesthetic.
Pose & Composition:

Pose: A high-angle 0.5x wide-angle POV shot looking directly down from a standing position. This perspective creates a realistic smartphone distortion that accentuates her fit hourglass silhouette and long, toned legs.
Geometry: She is standing slightly turned on the sand, with her long, voluminous chestnut hair flowing over one shoulder.
Environment & Lighting:

Lighting: Direct, bright natural daylight. The sun creates high-contrast highlights on the knit texture of the dress and casts sharp, defined shadows that define the anatomical depth of her silhouette.
Background (SHARP): A detailed sandy beach environment with visible footprints and coarse sand textures. Every grain of sand and every stitch of the dress is perfectly IN FOCUS. No artificial blur.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media vacation dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the shadows, 8k resolution.
```

## Battle-Hardened Medieval Warrior Cinematic Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, cinematic close-up portrait of a battle-hardened, elderly medieval warrior, facing slightly downward but locking eyes with the camera through piercing, cold steel-blue eyes full of intensity and defiance. His weathered, deeply wrinkled, sun-damaged skin shows countless scars, cuts, and dried blood stains, including a fresh stitched wound in an X shape on his forehead. His thick, unruly, salt-and-pepper beard and mustache frame his square, rugged jawline; his short, coarse grey hair is matted with sweat and dirt. Every pore, wrinkle, and skin imperfection is rendered with extreme detail. His nose is strong and slightly crooked, ears scarred and weathered from countless battles. His expression is stern, menacing, full of wisdom and pain, with furrowed brows and tightly compressed lips. His body is wrapped in a distressed, heavy brown wool cloak with frayed edges, partially covering dark medieval chainmail armor with metallic textures

reflecting subtle highlights. The background is dark and moody, pure black with a soft atmospheric haze to isolate the subject. Cinematic studio

lighting: single soft key light from the upper camera-left casting dramatic shadows across the face, subtle fill light from below to slightly illuminate the lower facial structure, creating extreme contrast and depth. Slight rim light touches the shoulders and beard edges for separation. Shot with a Canon EOS R5 + RF 85mm f/1.2 lens at f/1.2 for ultra-shallow depth of field, sharp razor focus only on the eyes, soft fall-off on beard and shoulders. ISO 100, shutter speed 1/250s.

Color palette: desaturated earth tones, deep browns, muted greys, blood reds, steel blacks. Cinematic color grading: teal and orange tones balanced for dramatic mood, heavy filmic shadows, with subtle fine-grain texture for realism. The image feels raw, emotional, like a poster frame from an epic dark fantasy film. --ar 3:4 --v 6.1 --style raw-quality 2 --stylize 50
```

## Impressionist Oil Painting of Rainy Urban Solitude

> Elle çizilmiş, fütüristik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-angle, wide-composition Impressionist oil painting of a rainy urban intersection. The scene is primarily monochrome with deep charcoal blacks and silvery grays, but features vibrant, colorful glowing lights at the footsteps of pedestrians crossing the street and beneath their umbrellas. A lone motorcyclist in the center leaves a trail of soft, colorful light, and car headlights emit small, brilliant pops of color. The style is heavily blurred and painterly, with loose, expressive brushstrokes and vague silhouettes, prioritizing mood and 'urban solitude' over sharp detail. The pavement is rain-soaked, reflecting both the gray sky and the neon-like colorful glows in shimmering, broken patterns. Misty atmosphere with distant buildings fading into soft tonal washes.
```

## Colossal Optimus Prime and Human Cinematic Photo Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyperrealistic cinematic photography, 2:3 aspect ratio, dynamic framing with a rotated side angle, never frontal. A person with a face and hair identical to the reference photo sits on the shoulder of a colossal Optimus Prime, perfectly integrated thanks to the same warm, epic sunset light. The scale emphasizes the human's smallness against the immensity of the Autobot leader.

The human figure wears realistic casual clothing: a basic T-shirt, light shirt, watch on the wrist, headphones around the neck, trousers, and simple footwear. Their clothing reflects the orange and red hues of the setting sun, blending seamlessly into the environment and creating a natural integration.

Optimus Prime dominates the scene: his metallic body, imposing and majestic, glows with intense red reflections from the sky in the flames of the setting sun. The plates of his armor display realistic textures of weathered metal, battle scratches, and minute mechanical details. His face is turned toward the horizon, conveying strength, nobility, and epic determination

The setting is a vast and dramatic landscape: a sunset sky ablaze with intense reds, oranges, and purples, clouds illuminated like fire on the horizon. Reflections bathe both Optimus's metal and the human's clothing, tinting the entire scene with warm, vibrant hues. The dusty ground reflects the reddish light, reinforcing the epic, cinematic atmosphere.

The composition conveys absolute hyperrealism, monumental scale, and epic drama, like a legendary instant where the human and the colossal unite under a blazing sunset
```

## Low-Quality Candid Smartphone Photo of Sadie Sink and Millie Bobby Brown

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Lower-quality smartphone photo of Sadie Sink and Millie Bobby Brown in a classroom, candid and joyful.

Sadie Sink stands on the left side of the classroom, wearing a beautiful crop top, relaxed and casual. She is laughing while sketching her character Max Mayfield from Stranger Things on a whiteboard using a marker. Her posture is playful and natural, mid-laugh, slightly turned toward Millie.

Millie Bobby Brown stands beside her at the same whiteboard, wearing a black tank top with a neckline. She is sketching her character Eleven from Stranger Things on the same board. Millie is also laughing, looking toward Sadie with a genuine, shared moment of humor.

The classroom feels real and ordinary: desks, chairs, backpacks, markers, and soft indoor lighting.
The whiteboard shows simple, hand-drawn sketches; casual, imperfect, clearly done for fun.

Captured as a low-quality smartphone photo with:

visible film grain

digital noise

slight blur and imperfect focus

uneven exposure

mild compression artifacts
Lighting is flat and mixed (daylight + classroom fluorescents).

Feels like a spontaneous behind-the-scenes moment, unposed, authentic, and friendly.
Ultra-realistic, no stylization.

Camera & Quality Cues

Older smartphone camera

Auto mode

Handheld framing

Slight motion softness

Washed colors

Natural skin texture

Negative Prompt:

Professional photoshoot, DSLR quality, studio lighting, sharp focus, HDR, glamour pose, airbrushed skin, CGI, illustration, anime, painting style, dramatic lighting, cinematic grading, distorted faces, extra fingers, watermark, text, logo
```

## Child's Crayon Drawing Style Generator

> Minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Draw {argument name="visual subject" default="[VISUAL]"} as if created by a cheerful 6-year-old on white notebook paper. Use chunky wax-crayon strokes, wobbly outlines, and simple flat shapes. Colors should be bright, bold, and slightly outside the lines, with visible pressure marks and small smudges. Perspective is innocent: objects can float, and size is based on importance, not realism. Faces are dots with curved smiles; hands are mitten blobs; animals are round and friendly. Add playful extras like a sun in a corner, puffy clouds, stars, and hearts. Include uneven scribble shading, a few tiny doodles in the margins, and one or two hand-written labels in childlike spelling.
```

```
- Preserve the original time-of-day feel and ambient light; keep it realistic and soft.\
- Ensure natural skin tones and fabric textures; no plastic smoothing.\
```

## Low-Quality Smartphone Photo of an Artist in a Classroom

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Low-quality smartphone photo of an extremely beautiful, attractive blonde woman with green eyes(attached woman) , standing next to her painting on a canvas.
The painting on the canvas is the attached image.

She is inside a classroom art studio, ordinary school setting with desks, easels, paint jars, and casual clutter.
She wears a white tank top with a deep neckline, visibly splattered with paint, along with casual pants also marked with paint stains.
A small paint is on her chest, subtle.
Paint smudges appear on her clothes, hands, and lightly on the floor around her workspace.

Her beauty is natural and believable: symmetrical facial features, expressive green eyes, realistic skin texture, minimal makeup.
Her pose is relaxed and confident, slightly angled toward the camera, with a quiet sense of pride as she stands beside her work. She smiles beautifully and elegantly.

Captured as a lower-quality smartphone photo:
noticeable digital noise, mild blur, imperfect focus, slight motion softness, compression artifacts, uneven white balance, and visible film grain.
Lighting is flat, indoor daylight mixed with classroom fluorescents.
Colors are slightly washed, not cinematic, clearly casual and unpolished—like a real student photo taken quickly.

Ultra-realistic, candid, no stylization.

Camera & Quality Cues

Older smartphone camera

Auto mode, imperfect exposure

Visible noise & grain

Slight blur / missed focus

JPEG compression artifacts

Flat indoor lighting

Negative Prompt:
High-end photography, studio lighting, professional DSLR, sharp focus, HDR, beauty filters, airbrushed skin, illustration, CGI, painting description, fantasy style, dramatic lighting, exaggerated anatomy, glamour shoot, watermark, text, logo
```

## Candid Classroom Photo with Anime Sketch on Whiteboard

> Fotogerçekçi, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use my reference image for face attachment 

A realistic, candid smartphone photograph with subtle film grain, taken inside a classroom. A large whiteboard dominates the scene. On the whiteboard is a hand-drawn sketch of {argument name="anime character" default="Nami from One Piece"}, drawn entirely with a blue whiteboard marker, loose but confident linework, clearly recognizable anime proportions. Nami is depicted wearing a deep-neckline tank top, stylized but appropriate for a casual sketch. 

Standing beside the whiteboard is Sadie Sink , casually posed and smiling toward the camera, as if proud of the drawing. She is wearing a deep-neckline tank top and fitted jeans. Natural posture, relaxed confidence. Classroom lighting is soft and slightly uneven, typical of overhead fluorescents. The photo has slight motion imperfections, realistic smartphone dynamic range, mild blur at the edges, and authentic film grain. Color tones are neutral with a hint of warmth. The overall mood feels spontaneous, informal, and behind-the-scenes. 

Style & Camera Details

Smartphone photography (iPhone/Android look)
35mm equivalent focal length
Natural indoor lighting
Subtle film grain, slight noise
Realistic skin tones
Documentary / candid aesthetic
Quality Cues
Ultra-realistic
High detail
Natural proportions
No studio lighting
Authentic classroom environment
Negative Prompt;
cartoonish rendering, over-sharpened, AI artifacts, exaggerated anatomy, glossy skin, unrealistic lighting, hypers*xualized pose, distorted hands, extra fingers, text artifacts, watermark, logo
```

## Cinematic Alpine Travel with Zootopia Characters

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic winter travel moment set at a quiet Alpine mountain station just after snowfall. A young man stands near the edge of a snow-dusted platform, wearing a matte black puffer jacket over a neutral sweater, dark jeans, and winter boots. His posture is relaxed, hands in pockets, breath faintly visible in the cold air, with a calm, confident expression and natural, realistic skin texture.

Beside him, {argument name="character 1" default="Nick Wilde from Zootopia"} leans against a metal railing, hands casually folded, wearing his signature green shirt and striped tie, giving a knowing side-smirk as if mid-conversation. A few steps away, {argument name="character 2" default="Judy Hopps"} stands atop a snow-covered luggage crate, adjusting her blue police uniform with an upbeat, alert expression, ears slightly raised.

In the background, towering snow-covered Alpine peaks fade into soft atmospheric haze. A red mountain train slowly departs the station, leaving subtle motion blur and a sense of journey. Cold winter daylight, soft diffused light reflecting off the snow, realistic shadows grounded in the environment. Ultra-detailed textures, crisp winter air, cinematic depth of field. Photorealistic human seamlessly blended with high-quality animated characters. Travel-film aesthetic, emotionally warm yet minimal, high resolution, 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

She is wearing a deep-neckline tank top and fitted jeans. Natural posture, relaxed confidence. Classroom lighting is soft and slightly uneven, typical of overhead fluorescents. The photo has slight motion imperfections, realistic smartphone dynamic range, mild blur at the edges, and authentic film grain. Color tones are neutral with a hint of warmth. The overall mood feels spontaneous, informal, and behind-the-scenes.

```
Style & Camera Details

Smartphone photography (iPhone/Android look)
35mm equivalent focal length

Quality Cues
```

## Surreal Video: Still Subject in Chaotic Flood

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Handheld smartphone camera perspective with subtle natural shake. A young woman with curly hair in a messy bun squats in front of a mirror, wearing a black vest over a white shirt and ripped blue jeans. She holds her phone toward the mirror, faintly smiling. Her body, face, and pose remain completely still for the entire video.
Suddenly, realistic clear water rapidly floods into the room from all directions. The water level rises quickly, submerging the bed, bookshelf, and plants. Water movement is fast and forceful, with visible ripples, bubbles, splashes, and floating particles. Light refracts through the water, creating shimmering reflections and distorted mirror visuals. The environment warps slightly due to water refraction.
The woman does not react. She remains frozen and calm while the surroundings move violently. Handheld framing stays consistent. Cinematic realism, high-detail water physics, dramatic lighting, surreal contrast between still subject and chaotic environment.
```

## Kine-Style Multi-Frame Japanese Night Festival Collage

> Sinematik, retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Edit image :: A stunning, high-fidelity image of five, seamless blend rectangle frames of varying sizes, capturing this subject in a Japanese night festival. The composition is masterfully balanced with a "Kine-style" or contemporary Japanese photography aesthetic, focusing on poetic lighting and negative space.
Frames and Poses:
 * Back-view Silhoutte: Arms raised in a V-sign, framed against a glowing Ferris wheel with golden light leaks.
 * Direct Gaze: Seated on industrial diamond-plate steps, bathed in soft, diffused blue-hour light.
 * Cinematic Profile: Pensive on concrete stairs with dramatic shadows and sharp highlights from a nearby neon sign.
 * Ethereal Lean: Against a white mesh fence, looking upward as warm bokeh from paper lanterns melts into the background.
 * Relaxed Recline: Lying back on wooden stairs, hands behind head, with prismatic lights leak from the upper edge frame.
Styling & Lighting:
She wears a white jersey tee under blue denim overal. The lighting is incredibly rich, featuring complex light play: prismatic lens flares, soft halation around crimson lanterns, and a moody chiaroscuro that highlights textures of concrete and steel.
Technical Aesthetic:
Shot on 35mm film ({argument name="film stock" default="Portra 400"} feel), the color grading is a sophisticated blend of desaturated teals and warm, nostalgic yellows. The images feature a fine, organic film grain, a gentle soft-focus bloom, and a deep, atmospheric "art house" richness. The atmosphere is electric yet intimate, blending the lively festival energy with quiet, soulful introspection.
```

## Quiet Luxury Editorial Photobook Spread

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Shot on Hasselblad X2D with 80mm lens, hyperrealistic printed photobook page of a Eurasian model, aged 20s, in an outdoor editorial setting.

LAYOUT: Hero + Multi-angle sidebar - Asymmetric editorial layout.

BOOK FORMAT & IMAGE PLACEMENT:
Open photobook spread viewed from a slight angled perspective, resting on a polished stone surface. Semi-gloss paper with visible grain, realistic ink reflection. Four-frame photobooth-style photo sequence in a precise 2×2 grid with thin black dividers.

HERO SECTION - STRICT INTERACTIVE FOUR-POSE SEQUENCE:
Top-left frame: Right cheek resting on right hand. Soft smile. Left hand loosely holding a {argument name="accessory" default="silk scarf"}. Head slightly turned to the right, eyes looking toward the top-right frame.

Top-right frame: Wearing the scarf as a head wrap. Right hand adjusting. One eye closed in a playful wink. Head slightly angled downward, eyes looking down toward the bottom-right frame.

Bottom-right frame: Holding the scarf near the chin. Subtle pout. Head slightly turned to the left, eyes looking toward the bottom-left frame.

Bottom-left frame: Scarf removed. Left arm lifted behind the head. Upper body turned slightly. Head lifted upward, eyes looking above the camera.

TONE: Tone-on-tone palette of creamy whites, soft beiges, and hints of sage green, quiet luxury aesthetic.

LIGHTING:
Soft, even studio lighting with natural skin tones and subtle shadows.

STYLE:
Quiet luxury editorial, natural movement, high fashion photography, old money aesthetic.

STYLE KEYWORDS:
Cream, light linen, natural light, subtle glam.

TYPOGRAPHY & DETAILS:
Minimal small text near the bottom of the page. Clean, understated sans-serif typography, with a tiny QR-style printed detail.

Negative prompt: illustration, doll, toy, stylization, CGI look, lens flare, chromatic aberration, JPEG artifacts.. A stylish handwritten signature Willy is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner
```

## Cinematic Winter Travel Scene with Zootopia Characters

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic winter travel moment set at a quiet Alpine mountain station just after snowfall. A young man stands near the edge of a snow-dusted platform, wearing a matte black puffer jacket over a neutral sweater, dark jeans, and winter boots. His posture is relaxed, hands in pockets, breath faintly visible in the cold air, with a calm, confident expression and natural, realistic skin texture.

Beside him, {argument name="animated character 1" default="Nick Wilde from Zootopia"} leans against a metal railing, hands casually folded, wearing his signature green shirt and striped tie, giving a knowing side-smirk as if mid-conversation. A few steps away, {argument name="animated character 2" default="Judy Hopps"} stands atop a snow-covered luggage crate, adjusting her blue police uniform with an upbeat, alert expression, ears slightly raised.

In the background, towering snow-covered Alpine peaks fade into soft atmospheric haze. A red mountain train slowly departs the station, leaving subtle motion blur and a sense of journey. Cold winter daylight, soft diffused light reflecting off the snow, realistic shadows grounded in the environment. Ultra-detailed textures, crisp winter air, cinematic depth of field. Photorealistic human seamlessly blended with high-quality animated characters. Travel-film aesthetic, emotionally warm yet minimal, high resolution, 4:5 aspect ratio.
```
